@src/model/mod.rs @src/config.rs @src/theme.rs @src/update/app.rs @src/commands.rs @src/messages.rs @Cargo.toml

I just implemented user configuration persistence for themes:

- EditorConfig in src/config.rs handles persistent settings with config.yaml
- Config directory is ~/.config/token-editor/ on all Unix (including macOS), %APPDATA%\token-editor\ on Windows
- get_config_dir() in src/theme.rs provides the cross-platform path using XDG_CONFIG_HOME or ~/.config fallback
- EditorConfig::ensure_config_dirs() creates the config directory structure on startup
- Theme selection persists to config.yaml and loads on startup with fallback to default-dark

The config structure:
- ~/.config/token-editor/config.yaml - editor settings (theme selection)
- ~/.config/token-editor/themes/ - user themes
- ~/.config/token-editor/keymap.yaml - user keybinding overrides

I simplified the loading to only have two layers: user config → embedded builtins (removed project-local loading).

AppModel::new() currently:
- Creates editor area with documents
- Loads files passed as command line args
- Calls EditorConfig::ensure_config_dirs() to create config folders
- Loads EditorConfig and theme on startup
- Has file loading logic mixed with UI setup

The file loading in AppModel::new is complex - it handles:
- Loading first file or creating demo document
- Opening additional files as tabs
- Error handling with fallback messages
- Creating editor state with viewport dimensions

For file dialogs, the app uses winit for windowing. Native file dialogs would need rfd crate or similar.

I told you I prefer XDG-style ~/.config paths over platform-native locations like ~/Library/Application Support on macOS.

next we should focus on saving/loading files, file dialog, refactor/cleanup the appmodel::new file loading and setup, consult the oracle and lets first plan out the features and changes we would like to do and try to combine these feateures with the features listed in  @docs/feature/workspace-management.md  in the back of our head, first we should create a basic version of file loading/saving with dialogs, then start work on supporting workspaces and adding the sidebar/panel/filetree concept later

1) TL;DR
- Refactor `AppModel::new()` into three clean phases: config/theme + geometry, initial documents/editor area, then UI/model assembly, with file loading factored into a helper.
- Integrate `rfd` via new `Cmd` variants (`ShowOpenFileDialog`, `ShowSaveFileDialog`, `ShowOpenFolderDialog`) executed in the winit loop, which in turn emit new `AppMsg` “dialog result” messages.
- Add only a minimal workspace notion now (e.g. `workspace_root: Option<PathBuf>`); defer the full `Workspace` struct/FileTree until you implement the sidebar.

Effort: S–M (2–4h) for refactor + dialogs; workspace sidebar later is a separate L–XL task.

## 1. Structure for the refactored `AppModel::new()`

Goal: clear separation: **startup config → file loading → UI initialization** and prepare for later `StartupConfig`/workspace support.

### a) Introduce a small geometry/helper type (pure, no I/O)

Add a helper to encapsulate the viewport calculations:

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct ViewportGeometry {
    pub window_width: u32,
    pub window_height: u32,
    pub line_height: usize,
    pub char_width: f32,
    pub visible_lines: usize,
    pub visible_columns: usize,
}

impl ViewportGeometry {
    pub fn new(window_width: u32, window_height: u32) -> Self {
        let line_height = 20;
        let char_width: f32 = 10.0;

        let text_x = crate::model::text_start_x(char_width).round();
        let visible_columns =
            ((window_width as f32 - text_x) / char_width).floor() as usize;

        let status_bar_height = line_height;
        let visible_lines = (window_height as usize)
            .saturating_sub(status_bar_height) / line_height;

        Self {
            window_width,
            window_height,
            line_height,
            char_width,
            visible_lines,
            visible_columns,
        }
    }
}
```

This keeps geometry math out of `AppModel::new()` and makes later reuse (e.g. from a `StartupConfig`) easier.

### b) Extract “load config + theme” into a helper

```rust
fn load_config_and_theme() -> (EditorConfig, Theme) {
    EditorConfig::ensure_config_dirs();

    let config = EditorConfig::load();
    let theme = load_theme(&config.theme).unwrap_or_else(|e| {
        tracing::warn!(
            "Failed to load theme '{}': {}, using default",
            config.theme, e
        );
        Theme::default()
    });

    (config, theme)
}
```

### c) Extract “create initial editor area + status message” into a helper

Move all `Document::from_file` and tab-creation logic into something like:

```rust
pub struct InitialSession {
    pub editor_area: EditorArea,
    pub status_message: String,
    pub workspace_root: Option<std::path::PathBuf>, // for later "Open Folder"/CLI
}

fn create_initial_session(
    file_paths: Vec<PathBuf>,
    geom: &ViewportGeometry,
) -> InitialSession {
    let (first_document, status_message) = if let Some(first_path) = file_paths.first() {
        match Document::from_file(first_path.clone()) {
            Ok(doc) => {
                let msg = if file_paths.len() > 1 {
                    format!("Opened {} files", file_paths.len())
                } else {
                    format!("Loaded: {}", first_path.display())
                };
                (doc, msg)
            }
            Err(e) => {
                let msg = format!("Error loading {}: {}", first_path.display(), e);
                (Document::new(), msg)
            }
        }
    } else {
        (Document::new(), "New file".to_string())
    };

    let editor = EditorState::with_viewport(geom.visible_lines, geom.visible_columns);
    let mut editor_area = EditorArea::single_document(first_document, editor);

    for path in file_paths.into_iter().skip(1) {
        // same logic currently in AppModel::new for extra tabs,
        // but reusing geom.visible_lines / visible_columns
        // ...
    }

    InitialSession {
        editor_area,
        status_message,
        workspace_root: None, // later: fill when starting from workspace root
    }
}
```

Later, when you introduce `StartupConfig` (CLI), you’ll likely change this to:

```rust
fn create_initial_session_from_startup(
    startup: &StartupConfig,
    geom: &ViewportGeometry,
) -> InitialSession { ... }
```

### d) Refactor `AppModel::new()` to orchestrate helpers

```rust
impl AppModel {
    pub fn new(window_width: u32, window_height: u32, file_paths: Vec<PathBuf>) -> Self {
        let geom = ViewportGeometry::new(window_width, window_height);
        let (config, theme) = load_config_and_theme();
        let InitialSession {
            editor_area,
            status_message,
            workspace_root,
        } = create_initial_session(file_paths, &geom);

        Self {
            editor_area,
            ui: UiState::with_status(status_message),
            theme,
            config,
            window_size: (window_width, window_height),
            line_height: geom.line_height,
            char_width: geom.char_width,
            // later: workspace_root,
            #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
            debug_overlay: Some(DebugOverlay::new()),
        }
    }
}
```

This meets your priority (1): file/document loading is now encapsulated and distinct from config/theme/UI setup, and you’re set up to swap `file_paths` for a richer `StartupConfig` later with minimal churn.

## 2. Integrating `rfd` dialogs with the winit event loop

You already have Elm-style `Cmd` for async I/O. Treat dialogs the same: **update → Cmd → side-effect (rfd) → AppMsg result**.

### a) Extend `Cmd` with dialog commands

In `commands.rs`:

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub enum Cmd {
    #[default]
    None,
    Redraw,
    SaveFile { path: PathBuf, content: String },
    LoadFile { path: PathBuf },
    Batch(Vec<Cmd>),

    // New:
    ShowOpenFileDialog {
        allow_multi: bool,
        start_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
    },
    ShowSaveFileDialog {
        suggested_path: Option<PathBuf>,
    },
    ShowOpenFolderDialog {
        start_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
    },
}
```

You keep dialogs out of your pure `update_*` functions; they only return these `Cmd`s.

### b) Add corresponding AppMsg “result” messages

In `messages.rs` `AppMsg`:

```rust
pub enum AppMsg {
    Resize(u32, u32),
    SaveFile,
    LoadFile(PathBuf),
    NewFile,
    SaveCompleted(Result<(), String>),
    FileLoaded { path: PathBuf, result: Result<String, String> },
    Quit,

    // New dialog-driven flows:
    SaveFileAs, // user requested "Save As..."
    SaveFileAsDialogResult {
        path: Option<PathBuf>, // None if user cancelled
    },

    OpenFileDialog, // user pressed Cmd+O / palette
    OpenFileDialogResult {
        paths: Vec<PathBuf>, // empty if cancel
    },

    OpenFolderDialog,
    OpenFolderDialogResult {
        folder: Option<PathBuf>,
    },
}
```

### c) Use `update_app` to map intent → dialog Cmds → follow-up work

Examples:

```rust
pub fn update_app(model: &mut AppModel, msg: AppMsg) -> Option<Cmd> {
    match msg {
        // Existing variants...

        AppMsg::SaveFileAs => {
            // Suggest current file name or something generic
            let suggested = model.document().file_path.clone();
            Some(Cmd::ShowSaveFileDialog { suggested_path: suggested })
        }

        AppMsg::SaveFileAsDialogResult { path } => {
            if let Some(path) = path {
                // Update model then trigger async save
                {
                    let doc = model.document_mut();
                    doc.file_path = Some(path.clone());
                }
                let content = model.document().buffer.to_string();
                model.ui.is_saving = true;
                model.ui.set_status("Saving...");

                Some(Cmd::SaveFile { path, content })
            } else {
                // User canceled
                model.ui.set_status("Save canceled");
                Some(Cmd::Redraw)
            }
        }

        AppMsg::OpenFileDialog => {
            let start_dir = model
                .document()
                .file_path
                .as_ref()
                .and_then(|p| p.parent().map(|p| p.to_path_buf()));
            Some(Cmd::ShowOpenFileDialog {
                allow_multi: true,
                start_dir,
            })
        }

        AppMsg::OpenFileDialogResult { paths } => {
            if paths.is_empty() {
                model.ui.set_status("Open file canceled");
                return Some(Cmd::Redraw);
            }

            // For now: open each file as a new tab using existing layout logic
            let mut cmds = Vec::new();
            for path in paths {
                cmds.push(
                    crate::update::update_layout(
                        model,
                        crate::messages::LayoutMsg::OpenFileInNewTab(path),
                    ).into()
                );
            }

            Some(Cmd::batch(cmds))
        }

        AppMsg::OpenFolderDialog => {
            Some(Cmd::ShowOpenFolderDialog {
                start_dir: None, // or from last workspace_root
            })
        }

        AppMsg::OpenFolderDialogResult { folder } => {
            if let Some(root) = folder {
                // Minimal workspace support: just remember the root for now
                model.ui.set_status(format!("Workspace root: {}", root.display()));
                // later: model.workspace_root = Some(root);
            } else {
                model.ui.set_status("Open folder canceled");
            }
            Some(Cmd::Redraw)
        }

        // existing branches...
    }
}
```

Note: In `OpenFileDialogResult` I sketched using `update_layout` directly; depending on how you want to structure that, you might instead emit `LayoutMsg` from the top-level `update` and not from `update_app`. The important bit is: dialog selects paths → store in `AppMsg` → open them using existing `EditorArea`/`LayoutMsg` machinery.

### d) Execute `Cmd` variants in the winit event loop with `rfd`

In your `main.rs` or wherever you run the Elm loop:

- You probably already have something like:

```rust
fn handle_cmd(cmd: Cmd, event_loop_proxy: &EventLoopProxy<Msg>, window: &winit::window::Window) {
    match cmd {
        Cmd::SaveFile { path, content } => { /* spawn task, then AppMsg::SaveCompleted */ }
        Cmd::LoadFile { path } => { /* spawn task, then AppMsg::FileLoaded */ }
        Cmd::Batch(cmds) => for c in cmds { handle_cmd(c, event_loop_proxy, window); }
        Cmd::Redraw => { /* mark need redraw */ }

        Cmd::ShowOpenFileDialog { allow_multi, start_dir } => {
            let mut dlg = rfd::FileDialog::new();
            if let Some(dir) = start_dir {
                dlg = dlg.set_directory(dir);
            }
            dlg = dlg.set_parent(window); // important on macOS

            let paths = if allow_multi {
                dlg.pick_files().unwrap_or_default()
            } else {
                dlg.pick_file().into_iter().collect()
            };

            let msg = Msg::App(AppMsg::OpenFileDialogResult { paths });
            let _ = event_loop_proxy.send_event(msg);
        }

        Cmd::ShowSaveFileDialog { suggested_path } => {
            let mut dlg = rfd::FileDialog::new().set_parent(window);
            if let Some(path) = suggested_path {
                if let Some(dir) = path.parent() {
                    dlg = dlg.set_directory(dir);
                }
                if let Some(name) = path.file_name() {
                    dlg = dlg.set_file_name(name);
                }
            }
            let selected = dlg.save_file();

            let msg = Msg::App(AppMsg::SaveFileAsDialogResult { path: selected });
            let _ = event_loop_proxy.send_event(msg);
        }

        Cmd::ShowOpenFolderDialog { start_dir } => {
            let mut dlg = rfd::FileDialog::new().set_parent(window);
            if let Some(dir) = start_dir {
                dlg = dlg.set_directory(dir);
            }
            let folder = dlg.pick_folder();

            let msg = Msg::App(AppMsg::OpenFolderDialogResult { folder });
            let _ = event_loop_proxy.send_event(msg);
        }

        Cmd::None => {}
    }
}
```

Key points:

- **Run the `rfd` calls on the main thread** inside the winit loop; they block, but native modal dialogs are expected to block the app’s message loop briefly, and OS keeps UI responsive.
- Use `set_parent(window)` on macOS/Windows so the dialog is properly modal/focused.
- Use `EventLoopProxy<Msg>` (or equivalent) to feed `Msg::App(...)` back into the Elm pipeline.

No async runtimes are required; you keep the same architectural pattern as your async file I/O commands.

## 3. Workspace struct now or later?

Given your priorities:

- You need **“Open Folder”** relatively soon (priority 4).
- Full workspace + file tree + watcher is a much larger scope (design doc shows a big `Workspace` and `FileTree`).

**Recommendation:**
Add only a **minimal field** to `AppModel` now, and defer the full `Workspace` struct until you implement the sidebar.

For example:

```rust
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct AppModel {
    pub editor_area: EditorArea,
    pub ui: UiState,
    pub theme: Theme,
    pub config: EditorConfig,
    pub window_size: (u32, u32),
    pub line_height: usize,
    pub char_width: f32,

    // minimal workspace support:
    pub workspace_root: Option<PathBuf>,

    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    pub debug_overlay: Option<DebugOverlay>,
}
```

And in `AppMsg::OpenFolderDialogResult`:

```rust
if let Some(root) = folder {
    model.workspace_root = Some(root.clone());
    model.ui.set_status(format!("Workspace root: {}", root.display()));
}
```

Later, when you introduce the real `Workspace` module:

- Replace `workspace_root: Option<PathBuf>` with `workspace: Option<Workspace>`.
- Add `WorkspaceMsg` and `update_workspace`.
- Reuse the existing “open folder” dialog + AppMsg to construct `Workspace::new(root)`.

This keeps today’s changes small and forward-compatible.

## 4. New messages/commands needed for dialogs

Here’s a concise checklist of what to add.

### a) Command palette / keymap commands (`CommandId`)

In `commands.rs`:

```rust
pub enum CommandId {
    // File operations
    NewFile,
    SaveFile,
    // New:
    SaveFileAs,
    OpenFile,
    OpenFolder,
    // ...
}
```

Add them to `COMMANDS` with macOS-ish defaults if you like:

- `OpenFile` → label "Open File..." (`⌘O`)
- `SaveFileAs` → label "Save File As..." (`⇧⌘S`)
- `OpenFolder` → label "Open Folder..." (e.g. `⌘K ⌘O` or whatever you prefer)

Map them into your `KeymapCommand` if/when needed.

Extend `execute_command` in `app.rs`:

```rust
pub fn execute_command(model: &mut AppModel, cmd_id: CommandId) -> Option<Cmd> {
    match cmd_id {
        CommandId::NewFile => update_layout(model, LayoutMsg::NewTab),
        CommandId::SaveFile => update_app(model, AppMsg::SaveFile),
        CommandId::SaveFileAs => update_app(model, AppMsg::SaveFileAs),
        CommandId::OpenFile => update_app(model, AppMsg::OpenFileDialog),
        CommandId::OpenFolder => update_app(model, AppMsg::OpenFolderDialog),
        // existing branches...
    }
}
```

### b) App-level messages (`AppMsg`)

New variants:

```rust
pub enum AppMsg {
    // existing...

    // Save As
    SaveFileAs,
    SaveFileAsDialogResult { path: Option<PathBuf> },

    // Open File
    OpenFileDialog,
    OpenFileDialogResult { paths: Vec<PathBuf> },

    // Open Folder / workspace root
    OpenFolderDialog,
    OpenFolderDialogResult { folder: Option<PathBuf> },
}
```

### c) Side-effect commands (`Cmd`)

New variants in `commands.rs`:

```rust
pub enum Cmd {
    // existing...
    ShowOpenFileDialog {
        allow_multi: bool,
        start_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
    },
    ShowSaveFileDialog {
        suggested_path: Option<PathBuf>,
    },
    ShowOpenFolderDialog {
        start_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
    },
}
```

These are the only new primitives you need; the rest of the logic stays in your existing Elm pipeline.

---

## 5. Risks and guardrails

- **Blocking the winit loop with dialogs:**
  Guardrail: only call `rfd` inside `Cmd` handling for explicit dialog commands; avoid using it directly in `update_*` or on hot paths. Modal dialogs are rare and acceptable to block on.

- **State desync on cancel:**
  Always model cancel as `Option<PathBuf>` (or empty `Vec<PathBuf>`) and handle it explicitly in `update_app` (set a status like "Open canceled" or simply do nothing). Never assume a dialog returns a path.

- **Workspace scope creep:**
  Do not introduce the full `Workspace`/`FileTree` types now. Keep `workspace_root: Option<PathBuf>` only and wire nothing else until you actually render the sidebar.

- **Signature churn for `AppModel::new()`:**
  Keep current `new(window_width, window_height, file_paths)` for now; when CLI is added, add a separate constructor or `from_startup_config` and migrate gradually rather than forcing a large refactor in one step.

---

## 6. When to consider the advanced path (and what it is)

Consider a more complex design if:

- Dialog usage grows (e.g. “Save All”, “Export…”, etc.) and you want **typed, composable dialog flows**.
- Startup becomes more complex (sessions, MRU, projects, etc.) and you want `AppModel::init` to return an initial `Cmd` batch to asynchronously load everything.
- You adopt an async runtime and want **non-blocking dialogs** (e.g. `rfd` async features with `tokio`).

**Advanced path outline (for later):**

- Replace ad-hoc dialog commands with a generic:

  ```rust
  pub enum FileDialogKind { OpenFile, SaveFile, OpenFolder }
  pub enum Cmd {
      ShowFileDialog { kind: FileDialogKind, options: FileDialogOptions },
      // ...
  }
  ```

- Introduce `StartupConfig` now and change `AppModel::new` to:

  ```rust
  pub fn init(window_size: (u32, u32), startup: StartupConfig) -> (AppModel, Cmd)
  ```

  where the `Cmd` loads initial files asynchronously (no synchronous `Document::from_file` on startup).

- Introduce a real `Workspace` struct + `WorkspaceMsg` as in your design doc, and migrate `workspace_root` into it, with `notify`-based file watching.

For now, the simple path above is enough to get clean initialization, native Save As/Open/Open Folder dialogs, and a minimal workspace root without committing to the full workspace system.
```

`Cmd::LoadFile` - Async commands for file I/O\n- `LayoutMsg::OpenFileInNewTab(path)` - Opens file as new tab\n- `WindowEvent::DroppedFile` handling - Basic drag-and-drop (opens in new tab)\n- Workspace concept with file tree sidebar\n- Duplicate file detection (already-open files)\n\n### Architecture\n\nThe editor follows Elm Architecture: `Message → Update → Command → Render`\n\n```\nUser Action → WindowEvent/Keypress\n    ↓\nMessage (Msg::App, Msg::Layout, etc.)\n    ↓\nupdate() → mutates AppModel, returns Option<Cmd>\n    ↓\nCommand execution (async I/O, dialogs)\n    ↓\nResult message → back to update()\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase 1: Refactor AppModel::new()\n\n**Goal:** Separate concerns - config/theme loading, file loading, UI initialization.\n\n### New Helper Types\n\n```rust\n// src/model/mod.rs or src/model/geometry.rs\n\n/// Viewport geometry calculations (pure, no I/O)\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\npub struct ViewportGeometry {\n    pub window_width: u32,\n    pub window_height: u32,\n    pub line_height: usize,\n    pub char_width: f32,\n    pub visible_lines: usize,\n    pub visible_columns: usize,\n}\n\nimpl ViewportGeometry {\n    pub fn new(window_width: u32, window_height: u32) -> Self {\n        let line_height = 20;\n        let char_width: f32 = 10.0;\n        let text_x = text_start_x(char_width).round();\n        let visible_columns = ((window_width as f32 - text_x) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n        let status_bar_height = line_height;\n        let visible_lines = (window_height as usize)\n            .saturating_sub(status_bar_height) / line_height;\n\n        Self { window_width, window_height, line_height, char_width, visible_lines, visible_columns }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Helper src/update/app.rs\n\nAppMsg::SaveFileAs => {\n    let suggested = model.document().file_path.clone();\n    Some(Cmd::ShowSaveFileDialog { suggested_path: suggested Add to Msg enum:\nDrop(DropMsg),\n```\n\n### Event Handling\n\n```rust\n// src/runtime/app.rs\n\nWindowEvent::HoveredFile(path) => {\n    update(&mut self.model, Msg::Drop(DropMsg::FileHovered(path.clone())))\n}\n\nWindowEvent::HoveredFileCancelled => {\n    update(&mut self.model, Msg::Drop(DropMsg::HoverCancelled))\n}\n\nWindowEvent::DroppedFile(path) => {\n    // Clear hover state and open file\n    update(&mut self.model, Msg::Drop(DropMsg::FilesDropped(vec![path.clone()])))\n}\n```\n\n### Update Handler\n\n```rust\n// src/update/drop.rs (new file)\n\npub fn update_drop(model: &mut AppModel, msg: DropMsg) -> Option<Cmd> {\n    match msg {\n        DropMsg::FileHovered(path) => {\n            model.ui.drop_state.hovered_files = vec![path];\n            model.ui.drop_state.is_hovering = true;\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n        \n        DropMsg::HoverCancelled => {\n            model.ui.drop_state.is_hovering = false;\n            model.ui.drop_state.hovered_files.clear();\n            Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n        }\n        \n        DropMsg::FilesDropped(paths) => {\n            model.ui.drop_state.is_hovering = false;\n            model.ui.drop_state.hovered_files.clear();\n            \n            // Open each file in a new tab\n            let cmds: Vec<Cmd> = paths.into_iter()\n                .map(|path| {\n                    update_layout(model, LayoutMsg::OpenFileInNewTab(path))\n                        .unwrap_or(Cmd::None)\n                })\n                .collect();\n            \n            Some(Cmd::batch(cmds))\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Theme Extension\n\n```rust\n// src/theme.rs\n\npub struct DropZoneTheme {\n    pub overlay: Color,  // Semi-transparent background\n    pub border: Color,   // Border highlight\n    pub text: Color,     // \"Drop to open\" text\n}\n\nimpl DropZoneTheme {\n    pub fn default_dark() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            overlay: Color::rgba(0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xB4),\n            border: Color::rgb(0x00, 0x7A, 0xCC),\n            text: Color::rgb(0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF),\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Rendering\n\n```rust\n// In renderer\n\nfn render_drop_overlay(&mut self, model: &AppModel) {\n    if !model.ui.drop_state.is_hovering {\n        return;\n    }\n    \n    // Draw semi-transparent overlay\n    // Draw border\n    // Draw centered text: \"Drop to open: filename\" or \"Drop to open N files\"\n}\n```\n\n### Tasks\n\n- [ ] Add `DropState` to `UiState`\n- [ ] Add `DropMsg` message type\n- [ ] Handle `WindowEvent::HoveredFile` and `HoveredFileCancelled`\n- [ ] Create `update_drop()` handler\n- [ ] Add `DropZoneTheme` to theme\n- [ ] Implement `render_drop_overlay()`\n- [ ] Update existing `DroppedFile` handling to use `DropMsg`\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4: File Validation\n\n**Goal:** Validate files before opening (binary detection, size limits, permissions).\n\n### Error Types\n\n```rust\n// src/model/document.rs or new file\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum FileOpenError {\n    NotFound,\n    PermissionDenied,\n    IsDirectory,\n    BinaryFile,\n    TooLarge { size_mb: f64 },\n    IoError(String),\n}\n\nimpl FileOpenError {\n    pub fn user_message(&self, filename: &str) -> String {\n        match self {\n            Self::NotFound => format!(\"File not found: {}\", filename),\n            Self::PermissionDenied => format!(\"Permission denied: {}\", filename),\n            Self::IsDirectory => format!(\"Cannot open directory: {}\", filename),\n            Self::BinaryFile => format!(\"Binary file: {}\", filename),\n            Self::TooLarge { size_mb } => format!(\"{} is too large ({:.1} MB)\", filename, size_mb),\n            Self::IoError(msg) => format!(\"Error opening {}: {}\", filename, msg),\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Validation Functions\n\n```rust\nconst MAX_FILE_SIZE: u64 = 50 * 1024 * 1024; // 50 MB\n\npub fn validate_file_for_opening(path: &Path) -> Result<(), FileOpenError> {\n    let metadata = std::fs::metadata(path).map_err(|e| match e.kind() {\n        std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => FileOpenError::NotFound,\n        std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => FileOpenError::PermissionDenied,\n        _ => FileOpenError::IoError(e.to_string()),\n    })?;\n\n    if metadata.is_dir() {\n        return Err(FileOpenError::IsDirectory);\n    }\n\n    if metadata.len() > MAX_FILE_SIZE {\n        return Err(FileOpenError::TooLarge {\n            size_mb: metadata.len() as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0)\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\npub fn is_binary_file(path: &Path) -> bool {\n    if let Ok(mut file) = std::fs::File::open(path) {\n        let mut buffer = [0u8; 8192];\n        if let Ok(n) = std::io::Read::read(&mut file, &mut buffer) {\n            return buffer[..n].contains(&0);\n        }\n    }\n    false\n}\n```\n\n### Integration\n\nUpdate `open_file_in_new_tab()` in `src/update/layout.rs`:\n\n```rust\nfn open_file_in_new_tab(model: &mut AppModel, path: PathBuf) {\n    // Validate first\n    if let Err(e) = validate_file_for_opening(&path) {\n        let filename = path.file_name().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy();\n        model.ui.set_status(e.user_message(&filename));\n        return;\n    }\n    \n    if is_binary_file(&path) {\n        let filename = path.file_name().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy();\n        model.ui.set_status(format!(\"Binary file: {}\", filename));\n        return;\n    }\n    \n    // ... existing loading logic\n}\n```\n\n### Tasks\n\n- [ ] Add `FileOpenError` enum\n- [ ] Implement `validate_file_for_opening()`\n- [ ] Implement `is_binary_file()`\n- [ ] Integrate validation into `open_file_in_new_tab()`\n- [ ] Add user-friendly error messages to status bar\n\n---\n\n## Phase 5: CLI Arguments with clap\n\n**Goal:** Parse command-line arguments with flags for startup behavior.\n\n### Dependencies\n\n```toml\n# Cargo.toml\nclap = { version = \"4\", features = [\"derive\"] }\n```\n\n### CLI Specification\n\n```\nUSAGE:\n    token [OPTIONS] [PATHS]...\n\nARGS:\n    [PATHS]...    Files or directories to open\n\nOPTIONS:\n    -n, --new              Start with empty buffer (ignore session)\n    -w, --wait             Wait for all files to close (for git/svn)\n    --line <N>             Go to line N in first file\n    --column <N>           Go to column N (with --line)\n    -h, --help             Print help information\n    -V, --version          Print version information\n```\n\n### Data Structures\n\n```rust\n// src/cli.rs (new file)\n\nuse clap::Parser;\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\n\n#[derive(Parser, Debug)]\n#[command(name = \"token\", version, about = \"A fast text editor\")]\npub struct CliArgs {\n    /// Files or directories to open\n    #[arg(value_name = \"PATHS\")]\n    pub paths: Vec<PathBuf>,\n\n    /// Start with empty buffer\n    #[arg(short = 'n', long)]\n    pub new: bool,\n\n    /// Wait for files to close (git integration)\n    #[arg(short = 'w', long)]\n    pub wait: bool,\n\n    /// Go to line N in first file\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub line: Option<usize>,\n\n    /// Go to column N (with --line)\n    #[arg(long)]\n    pub column: Option<usize>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct StartupConfig {\n    pub mode: StartupMode,\n    pub initial_position: Option<(usize, usize)>,\n    pub wait_mode: bool,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum StartupMode {\n    Empty,\n    SingleFile(PathBuf),\n    MultipleFiles(Vec<PathBuf>),\n    Workspace { root: PathBuf, initial_files: Vec<PathBuf> },\n}\n\nimpl CliArgs {\n    pub fn into_config(self) -> Result<StartupConfig, String> {\n        let mode = if self.new || self.paths.is_empty() {\n            StartupMode::Empty\n        } else if self.paths.len() == 1 {\n            let path = &self.paths[0];\n            if path.is_dir() {\n                StartupMode::Workspace { root: path.clone(), initial_files: vec![] }\n            } else {\n                StartupMode::SingleFile(path.clone())\n            }\n        } else {\n            let (dirs, files): (Vec<_>, Vec<_>) = self.paths.iter()\n                .partition(|p| p.is_dir());\n            \n            if dirs.len() > 1 {\n                return Err(\"Cannot open multiple directories\".to_string());\n            }\n            \n            if let Some(dir) = dirs.first() {\n                StartupMode::Workspace {\n                    root: (*dir).clone(),\n                    initial_files: files.into_iter().cloned().collect(),\n                }\n            } else {\n                StartupMode::MultipleFiles(files.into_iter().cloned().collect())\n            }\n        };\n\n        Ok(StartupConfig {\n            mode,\n            initial_position: self.line.map(|l| (l, self.column.unwrap_or(0))),\n            wait_mode: self.wait,\n        })\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Integration\n\n```rust\n// src/main.rs\n\nfn main() {\n    let args = CliArgs::parse();\n    let startup = args.into_config().expect(\"Invalid arguments\");\n    \n    // Use startup.mode to determine what to open\n    // Use startup.initial_position to set cursor after loading\n    // Use startup.wait_mode for git integration behavior\n}\n```\n\n### Tasks\n\n- [ ] Add `clap` to Cargo.toml\n- [ ] Create `src/cli.rs` module\n- [ ] Implement `CliArgs` with derive macros\n- [ ] Implement `StartupConfig` and `StartupMode`\n- [ ] Update `main()` to use CLI parsing\n- [ ] Handle `--line` and `--column` flags\n- [ ] Handle `--wait` mode for git integration\n\n---\n\n## Phase 6: Workspace & FileTree Sidebar\n\n**Goal:** Full workspace support with file tree sidebar.\n\nSee [workspace-management.md](workspace-management.md) for detailed specification.\n\n### Summary\n\n- `Workspace` struct with `FileTree`, expanded folders, sidebar state\n- `WorkspaceMsg` for sidebar interactions\n- `update_workspace()` handler\n- Sidebar rendering with file icons\n- File system watching with `notify` crate\n- Mouse and keyboard navigation\n\n### Dependencies\n\n```toml\n# Cargo.toml\nnotify = \"6.1\"\n```\n\n### Tasks\n\n- [ ] Create `src/model/workspace.rs`\n- [ ] Implement `Workspace`, `FileTree`, `FileNode`\n- [ ] Add `WorkspaceMsg` to messages\n- [ ] Implement `update_workspace()`\n- [ ] Add sidebar rendering\n- [ ] Implement mouse hit-testing for sidebar\n- [ ] Add keyboard navigation (arrows, enter)\n- [ ] Integrate `notify` for file watching\n- [ ] Add Cmd+B to toggle sidebar\n\n---\n\n## Success Criteria\n\n### Phase 1 (Refactor)\n- [ ] `AppModel::new()` is clean with separated concerns\n- [ ] `ViewportGeometry` calculates dimensions\n- [ ] `workspace_root` field exists for future use\n\n### Phase 2 (Dialogs)\n- [ ] ⌘O opens native Open File dialog\n- [ ] ⇧⌘S opens native Save As dialog\n- [ ] ⇧⌘O opens native Open Folder dialog\n- [ ] Dialogs work on macOS, Linux, Windows\n- [ ] Commands appear in command palette\n\n### Phase 3 (Drop Feedback)\n- [ ] Dragging files shows overlay with file names\n- [ ] Overlay disappears when drag leaves window\n- [ ] Dropping opens files (existing behavior preserved)\n\n### Phase 4 (Validation)\n- [ ] Binary files show error message\n- [ ] Large files (>50MB) show error with size\n- [ ] Missing files show \"not found\" error\n- [ ] Permission errors are caught\n\n### Phase 5 (CLI)\n- [ ] `token file.rs` opens file\n- [ ] `token --new` starts with empty buffer\n- [ ] `token --line 42 file.rs` opens at line 42\n- [ ] `token ./src` opens directory as workspace\n\n### Phase 6 (Workspace)\n- [ ] Sidebar shows file tree\n- [ ] Folders expand/collapse\n- [ ] Clicking file opens in tab\n- [ ] Cmd+B toggles sidebar\n- [ ] External file changes update tree\n"

{\n10:     Up,\n11:     Down,\n12:     Left,\n13:     Right,\n14: }\n15: \n16: /// Editor-specific messages (cursor movement, viewport scrolling)\n17: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n18: pub enum EditorMsg {\n19:     // === Basic Movement ===\n20:     /// Move cursor in a direction\n21:     MoveCursor(Direction),\n22:     /// Move cursor to start of line (Home key)\n23:     MoveCursorLineStart,\n24:     /// Move cursor to end of line (End key)\n25:     MoveCursorLineEnd,\n26:     /// Move cursor to start of document (Ctrl+Home)\n27:     MoveCursorDocumentStart,\n28:     /// Move cursor to end of document (Ctrl+End)\n29:     MoveCursorDocumentEnd,\n30:     /// Move cursor by word (Option+Left/Right on Mac)\n31:     MoveCursorWord(Direction),\n32:     /// Page up\n33:     PageUp,\n34:     /// Page down\n35:     PageDown,\n36:     /// Set cursor to specific position (from mouse click)\n37:     SetCursorPosition { line: usize, column: usize },\n38:     /// Scroll viewport vertically (positive = down, negative = up)\n39:     Scroll(i32),\n40:     /// Scroll viewport horizontally (positive = right, negative = left)\n41:     ScrollHorizontal(i32),\n42: \n43:     // === Selection Movement (Shift+key) ===\n44:     /// Move cursor with selection (Shift+Arrow)\n45:     MoveCursorWithSelection(Direction),\n46:     /// Move to line start with selection (Shift+Home)\n47:     MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection,\n48:     /// Move to line end with selection (Shift+End)\n49:     MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection,\n50:     /// Move to document start with selection (Shift+Ctrl+Home)\n51:     MoveCursorDocumentStartWithSelection,\n52:     /// Move to document end with selection (Shift+Ctrl+End)\n53:     MoveCursorDocumentEndWithSelection,\n54:     /// Move word with selection (Shift+Option+Arrow)\n55:     MoveCursorWordWithSelection(Direction),\n56:     /// Page up with selection\n57:     PageUpWithSelection,\n58:     /// Page down with selection\n59:     PageDownWithSelection,\n60: \n61:     // === Selection Commands ===\n62:     /// Select all text (Cmd+A)\n63:     SelectAll,\n64:     /// Select word at cursor (double-click)\n65:     SelectWord,\n66:     /// Select entire line (triple-click)\n67:     SelectLine,\n68:     /// Extend selection to position (Shift+Click)\n69:     ExtendSelectionToPosition { line: usize, column: usize },\n70:     /// Clear all selections (collapse to cursors)\n71:     ClearSelection,\n72: \n73:     // === Multi-Cursor ===\n74:     /// Toggle cursor at position (Option+Click)\n75:     ToggleCursorAtPosition { line: usize, column: usize },\n76:     /// Add cursor above current (Option+Option+Up)\n77:     AddCursorAbove,\n78:     /// Add cursor below current (Option+Option+Down)\n79:     AddCursorBelow,\n80:     /// Collapse to single cursor (Escape with multiple cursors)\n81:     CollapseToSingleCursor,\n82:     /// Remove cursor by index\n83:     RemoveCursor(usize),\n84: \n85:     // === Find & Select ===\n86:     /// Select next occurrence of word/selection (Cmd+J)\n87:     SelectNextOccurrence,\n88:     /// Unselect last added occurrence (Shift+Cmd+J)\n89:     UnselectOccurrence,\n90:     /// Select all occurrences (Cmd+Shift+L)\n91:     SelectAllOccurrences,\n92: \n93:     // === Expand/Shrink Selection ===\n94:     /// Expand selection to next semantic level (Option+Up)\n95:     /// Progression: cursor → word → line → all\n96:     ExpandSelection,\n97:     /// Shrink selection to previous level (Option+Down)\n98:     /// Restores previous selection from history stack\n99:     ShrinkSelection,\n100: \n101:     // === Rectangle Selection (Middle mouse) ===\n102:     /// Start rectangle selection at position\n103:     StartRectangleSelection { line: usize, column: usize },\n104:     /// Update rectangle selection to position\n105:     UpdateRectangleSelection { line: usize, column: usize },\n106:     /// Finish rectangle selection\n107:     FinishRectangleSelection,\n108:     /// Cancel rectangle selection\n109:     CancelRectangleSelection,\n110: }\n111: \n112: /// Document-specific messages (text editing, undo/redo)\n113: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n114: pub enum DocumentMsg {\n115:     /// Insert a character at cursor\n116:     InsertChar(char),\n117:     /// Insert a newline at cursor\n118:     InsertNewline,\n119:     /// Delete character before cursor (Backspace)\n120:     DeleteBackward,\n121:     /// Delete word before cursor (Option+Backspace)\n122:     DeleteWordBackward,\n123:     /// Delete word after cursor (Option+Delete)\n124:     DeleteWordForward,\n125:     /// Delete character at cursor (Delete)\n126:     DeleteForward,\n127:     /// Delete entire current line (Cmd+Backspace)\n128:     DeleteLine,\n129:     /// Undo last edit\n130:     Undo,\n131:     /// Redo last undone edit\n132:     Redo,\n133:     /// Copy selection to clipboard (Cmd+C)\n134:     Copy,\n135:     /// Cut selection to clipboard (Cmd+X)\n136:     Cut,\n137:     /// Paste from clipboard (Cmd+V)\n138:     Paste,\n139:     /// Duplicate current line or selection (Cmd+D)\n140:     Duplicate,\n141:     /// Indent selected lines (Tab with selection)\n142:     IndentLines,\n143:     /// Unindent current line or selected lines (Shift+Tab)\n144:     UnindentLines,\n145: }\n146: \n147: use crate::model::{GroupId, ModalId, SegmentContent, SegmentId, SplitDirection, TabId};\n148: \n149: /// Modal-specific messages (command palette, goto line, find/replace)\n150: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n151: pub enum ModalMsg {\n152:     /// Open command palette\n153:     OpenCommandPalette,\n154:     /// Open goto line dialog\n155:     OpenGotoLine,\n156:     /// Open find/replace dialog\n157:     OpenFindReplace,\n158:     /// Close the currently active modal\n159:     Close,\n160:     /// Update modal input text\n161:     SetInput(String),\n162:     /// Insert character into modal input\n163:     InsertChar(char),\n164:     /// Delete character from modal input (backspace)\n165:     DeleteBackward,\n166:     /// Delete word backward from modal input (Option+Backspace)\n167:     DeleteWordBackward,\n168:     /// Move cursor word left in modal input (Option+Left)\n169:     MoveCursorWordLeft,\n170:     /// Move cursor word right in modal input (Option+Right)\n171:     MoveCursorWordRight,\n172:     /// Move selection up in list (e.g., command palette results)\n173:     SelectPrevious,\n174:     /// Move selection down in list\n175:     SelectNext,\n176:     /// Confirm/execute the modal action (Enter)\n177:     Confirm,\n178: }\n179: \n180: /// UI-specific messages (status bar, cursor blink, modals)\n181: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n182: pub enum UiMsg {\n183:     /// Set status bar message (legacy, for backward compatibility)\n184:     SetStatus(String),\n185:     /// Toggle cursor blink state\n186:     BlinkCursor,\n187:     /// Update a specific status bar segment\n188:     UpdateSegment {\n189:         id: SegmentId,\n190:         content: SegmentContent,\n191:     },\n192:     /// Set a transient message that auto-expires\n193:     SetTransientMessage { text: String, duration_ms: u64 },\n194:     /// Clear the transient message\n195:     ClearTransientMessage,\n196:     /// Modal messages\n197:     Modal(ModalMsg),\n198:     /// Toggle a modal (open if closed, close if open)\n199:     ToggleModal(ModalId),\n200: }\n201: \n202: /// Layout messages (split views, tabs, groups)\n203: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n204: pub enum LayoutMsg {\n205:     /// Create a new untitled document in the focused group\n206:     NewTab,\n207: \n208:     /// Open a file in a new tab in the focused group\n209:     OpenFileInNewTab(PathBuf),\n210: \n211:     /// Split the focused group in the given direction\n212:     /// Creates a new group with a copy of the current editor view\n213:     SplitFocused(SplitDirection),\n214: \n215:     /// Split a specific group in the given direction\n216:     SplitGroup {\n217:         group_id: GroupId,\n218:         direction: SplitDirection,\n219:     },\n220: \n221:     /// Close a group (and all its tabs)\n222:     /// If this is the last group, does nothing\n223:     CloseGroup(GroupId),\n224: \n225:     /// Close the focused group\n226:     CloseFocusedGroup,\n227: \n228:     /// Focus a specific group\n229:     FocusGroup(GroupId),\n230: \n231:     /// Focus the next group (cycle through groups)\n232:     FocusNextGroup,\n233: \n234:     /// Focus the previous group (cycle through groups)\n235:     FocusPrevGroup,\n236: \n237:     /// Focus group by index (1-indexed for keyboard shortcuts)\n238:     FocusGroupByIndex(usize),\n239: \n240:     /// Move a tab to a different group\n241:     MoveTab { tab_id: TabId, to_group: GroupId },\n242: \n243:     /// Close a specific tab\n244:     CloseTab(TabId),\n245: \n246:     /// Close the active tab in the focused group\n247:     CloseFocusedTab,\n248: \n249:     /// Switch to next tab in focused group\n250:     NextTab,\n251: \n252:     /// Switch to previous tab in focused group\n253:     PrevTab,\n254: \n255:     /// Switch to tab by index in focused group (0-indexed)\n256:     SwitchToTab(usize),\n257: }\n258: \n259: /// Application-level messages (file operations, window events)\n260: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n261: pub enum AppMsg {\n262:     /// Window resized\n263:     Resize(u32, u32),\n264:     /// Save current file\n265:     SaveFile,\n266:     /// Load a file\n267:     LoadFile(PathBuf),\n268:     /// Create a new file\n269:     NewFile,\n270:     /// File save completed (async result)\n271:     SaveCompleted(Result<(), String>),\n272:     /// File load completed (async result)\n273:     FileLoaded {\n274:         path: PathBuf,\n275:         result: Result<String, String>,\n276:     },\n277:     /// Quit the application\n278:     Quit,\n279: }\n280: \n281: /// Top-level message type\n282: #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n283: pub enum Msg {\n284:     /// Editor messages (cursor, viewport)\n285:     Editor(EditorMsg),\n286:     /// Document messages (text editing)\n287:     Document(DocumentMsg),\n288:     /// UI messages (status, animation)\n289:     Ui(UiMsg),\n290:     /// Layout messages (splits, tabs, groups)\n291:     Layout(LayoutMsg),\n292:     /// App messages (file I/O, window)\n293:     App(AppMsg),\n294: }\n295: \n296: // Convenience constructors for common messages\n297: impl Msg {\n298:     /// Create a cursor movement message\n299:     pub fn move_cursor(direction: Direction) -> Self {\n300:         Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::MoveCursor(direction))\n301:     }\n302: \n303:     /// Create an insert character message\n304:     pub fn insert_char(ch: char) -> Self {\n305:         Msg::Document(DocumentMsg::InsertChar(ch))\n306:     }\n307: \n308:     /// Create a resize message\n309:     pub fn resize(width: u32, height: u32) -> Self {\n310:         Msg::App(AppMsg::Resize(width, height))\n311:     }\n312: }",

}

This module provides a data-driven keybinding system that:\n4: //! - Maps keystrokes to editor commands\n5: //! - Supports platform-specific modifier handling (Cmd on macOS, Ctrl elsewhere)\n6: //! - Enables user customization via YAML config files\n7: //! - Supports multi-key sequences/chords\n8: //!\n9: //! # Architecture\n10: //!\n11: //! ```text\n12: //! winit::KeyEvent → Keystroke → Keymap::lookup() → Command → Vec<Msg>\n13: //! ```\n14: //!\n15: //! # Loading Keymaps\n16: //!\n17: //! ```ignore\n18: //! // Load from embedded defaults\n19: //! let keymap = Keymap::with_bindings(default_bindings());\n20: //!\n21: //! // Or load from YAML file\n22: //! let keymap = Keymap::load_from_yaml(\"keymap.yaml\")?;\n23: //! ```\n24: \n25: mod binding;\n26: mod command;\n27: mod config;\n28: mod context;\n29: mod defaults;\n30: #[allow(clippy::module_inception)]\n31: mod keymap;\n32: mod types;\n33: mod winit_adapter;\n34: \n35: pub use binding::Keybinding;\n36: pub use command::Command;\n37: pub use config::{load_keymap_file, parse_keymap_yaml, KeymapError};\n38: pub use context::{Condition, KeyContext};\n39: pub use defaults::{default_bindings, get_default_keymap_yaml, load_default_keymap, merge_bindings};\n40: pub use keymap::{KeyAction, Keymap};\n41: pub use types::{KeyCode, Keystroke, Modifiers};\n42: pub use winit_adapter::keystroke_from_winit;\n43: \n44: #[cfg(test)]\n45: mod tests;",

\n41:         AppMsg::NewFile => {\n42:             // TODO: Implement new file\n43:             model.ui.set_status(\"New file not yet implemented\");\n44:             Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n45:         }\n46: \n47:         AppMsg::SaveCompleted(result) => {\n48:             model.ui.is_saving = false;\n49:             match result {\n50:                 Ok(_) => {\n51:                     model.document_mut().is_modified = false;\n52:                     if let Some(path) = &model.document().file_path {\n53:                         model.ui.set_status(format!(\"Saved: {}\", path.display()));\n54:                     }\n55:                 }\n56:                 Err(e) => {\n57:                     model.ui.set_status(format!(\"Error: {}\", e));\n58:                 }\n59:             }\n60:             Some(Cmd::Redraw)\n61:         }\n62: \n63:         AppMsg::FileLoaded { path, result } => {\n64:             model.ui.is_loading = false;\n65:             match model.document_mut().redo_stack.clear();\n72:                     *model.editor_mut().primary_cursor_mut() = Default::default();\n73:                     model.ui.set_status(format!(\"Loaded: {}\", ## Assistant

Phases 1 & 2 are complete. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

### Phase 1: Refactored AppModel::new()
- Added `ViewportGeometry` struct for pure dimension calculations
- Extracted `load_config_and_theme()` helper function
- Extracted `create_initial_session()` for file loading logic
- Added `workspace_root: Option<PathBuf>` to `AppModel` for future workspace support
- Clean separation of concerns in `AppModel::new()`

### Phase 2: File Dialogs with rfd
- Added `rfd = "0.15"` dependency
- New `Cmd` variants: `ShowOpenFileDialog`, `ShowSaveFileDialog`, `ShowOpenFolderDialog`
- New `AppMsg` variants: `SaveFileAs`, `OpenFileDialog`, `OpenFolderDialog` + their result handlers
- New `CommandId` entries: `OpenFile`, `OpenFolder`, `SaveFileAs`
- Keybindings: ⌘O (Open File), ⇧⌘O (Open Folder), ⇧⌘S (Save As)
- Updated `keymap.yaml` and keymap command parser
- Dialog execution runs in background threads, sends results back via message channel

**New keybindings:**
- ⌘O → Open File dialog
- ⇧⌘O → Open Folder dialog
- ⇧⌘S → Save File As dialog

Shall I continue with Phase 3 (visual feedback for file dropping) or Phase 4 (file validation)?

  "content": "//! File validation utilities for opening files\n//!\n//! Validates files before attempting to open them, checking for:\n//! - File existence and permissions\n//! - File size limits\n//! - Binary file detection\n\nuse std::fs::{self, File};\nuse std::io::Read;\nuse std::path::Path;\n\n/// Maximum file size in bytes (50 MB)\npub const MAX_FILE_SIZE: u64 = 50 * 1024 * 1024;\n\n/// Errors that can occur when validating a file for opening\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum FileOpenError {\n    /// File does not exist\n    NotFound,\n    /// Permission denied to read file\n    PermissionDenied,\n    /// Path is a directory, not a file\n    IsDirectory,\n    /// File appears to be binary (contains null bytes)\n    BinaryFile,\n    /// File exceeds size limit\n    TooLarge { size_mb: f64 },\n    /// Other I/O error\n    IoError(String),\n}\n\nimpl FileOpenError {\n    /// Get a user-friendly error message\n    pub fn user_message(&self, filename: &str) -> String {\n        match self {\n            Self::NotFound => format!(\"File not found: {}\", filename),\n            Self::PermissionDenied => format!(\"Permission denied: {}\", filename),\n            Self::IsDirectory => format!(\"Cannot open directory: {}\", filename),\n            Self::BinaryFile => format!(\"Cannot open binary file: {}\", filename),\n            Self::TooLarge { size_mb } => {\n                format!(\"{} is too large ({:.1} MB, max {} MB)\", filename, size_mb, MAX_FILE_SIZE / (1024 * 1024))\n            }\n            Self::IoError(msg) => format!(\"Error opening {}: {}\", filename, msg),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl std::fmt::Display for FileOpenError {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {\n        match self {\n            Self::NotFound => write!(f, \"file not found\"),\n            Self::PermissionDenied => write!(f, \"permission denied\"),\n            Self::IsDirectory => write!(f, \"is a directory\"),\n            Self::BinaryFile => write!(f, \"binary file\"),\n            Self::TooLarge { size_mb } => write!(f, \"file too large ({:.1} MB)\", size_mb),\n            Self::IoError(msg) => write!(f, \"{}\", msg),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl std::error::Error for FileOpenError {}\n\n/// Validate a file before attempting to open it\n///\n/// Checks:\n/// - File exists\n/// - Is not a directory\n/// - Has read permissions\n/// - Does not exceed size limit\n///\n/// Does NOT check for binary content (use `is_likely_binary` separately after this passes)\npub fn validate_file_for_opening(path: &Path) -> Result<(), FileOpenError> {\n    let metadata = fs::metadata(path).map_err(|e| match e.kind() {\n        std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => FileOpenError::NotFound,\n        std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied => FileOpenError::PermissionDenied,\n        _ => FileOpenError::IoError(e.to_string()),\n    })?;\n\n    if metadata.is_dir() {\n        return Err(FileOpenError::IsDirectory);\n    }\n\n    if metadata.len() > MAX_FILE_SIZE {\n        return Err(FileOpenError::TooLarge {\n            size_mb: metadata.len() as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Check if a file is likely binary by scanning for null bytes\n///\n/// Reads the first 8KB of the file and checks for null bytes,\n/// which are common in binary files but rare in text files.\n///\n/// Returns `true` if the file appears to be binary, `false` if it appears to be text.\n/// Returns `false` on any read error (let the actual open fail with a better error).\npub fn is_likely_binary(path: &Path) -> bool {\n    let Ok(mut file) = File::open(path) else {\n        return false;\n    };\n\n    let mut buffer = [0u8; 8192];\n    let Ok(bytes_read) = file.read(&mut buffer) else {\n        return false;\n    };\n\n    // Check for null bytes in the read content\n    buffer[..bytes_read].contains(&0)\n}\n\n/// Get the filename from a path for display in error messages\npub fn filename_for_display(path: &Path) -> String {\n    path.file_name()\n        .unwrap_or_default()\n        .to_string_lossy()\n        .to_string()\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::io::Write;\n    use tempfile::NamedTempFile;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_validate_nonexistent_file() {\n        let result = validate_file_for_opening(Path::new(\"/nonexistent/path/file.txt\"));\n        assert!(matches!(result, Err(FileOpenError::NotFound)));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_validate_directory() {\n        let result = validate_file_for_opening(Path::new(\"/tmp\"));\n        assert!(matches!(result, Err(FileOpenError::IsDirectory)));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_validate_valid_file() {\n        let temp = NamedTempFile::new().unwrap();\n        let result = validate_file_for_opening(temp.path());\n        assert!(result.is_ok());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_is_binary_text_file() {\n        let mut temp = NamedTempFile::new().unwrap();\n        writeln!(temp, \"Hello, world!\").unwrap();\n        writeln!(temp, \"This is a text file.\").unwrap();\n        temp.flush().unwrap();\n\n        assert!(!is_likely_binary(temp.path()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_is_binary_with_null_bytes() {\n        let mut temp = NamedTempFile::new().unwrap();\n        temp.write_all(b\"Hello\\x00World\").unwrap();\n        temp.flush().unwrap();\n\n        assert!(is_likely_binary(temp.path()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_error_messages() {\n        assert_eq!(\n            FileOpenError::NotFound.user_message(\"test.txt\"),\n            \"File not found: test.txt\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            FileOpenError::IsDirectory.user_message(\"mydir\"),\n            \"Cannot open directory: mydir\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            FileOpenError::BinaryFile.user_message(\"image.png\"),\n            \"Cannot open binary file: image.png\"\n        );\n    }\n}\n"
}

## Assistant

Phase 4 is complete. Here's a summary:

### Phase 4: File Validation

Created `src/util/file_validation.rs` with:

- **`FileOpenError`** enum with variants:
  - `NotFound` - file doesn't exist
  - `PermissionDenied` - can't read file
  - `IsDirectory` - path is a directory
  - `BinaryFile` - file contains null bytes
  - `TooLarge { size_mb }` - exceeds 50 MB limit
  - `IoError(String)` - other I/O errors

- **`validate_file_for_opening(path)`** - validates existence, permissions, size
- **`is_likely_binary(path)`** - scans first 8KB for null bytes
- **`filename_for_display(path)`** - extracts filename for error messages
- **`MAX_FILE_SIZE`** constant (50 MB)

Integrated validation into:
- `open_file_in_new_tab()` in `src/update/layout.rs` - validates before opening files from dialogs/drag-drop
- `create_initial_session()` in `src/model/mod.rs` - validates CLI-provided files at startup

Also reorganized `src/util.rs` → `src/util/mod.rs` with submodules:
- `src/util/text.rs` - existing text utilities
- `src/util/file_validation.rs` - new validation module

Would you like me to continue with Phase 3 (visual feedback for file dropping)?

`LayoutMsg::OpenFileInNewTab(path)` - Opens file as new tab\n16: - `WindowEvent::DroppedFile` handling - Basic drag-and-drop (opens in new tab)\n17: - `EditorConfig` persistence to `~/.config/token-editor/config.yaml`\n18: - Theme loading with user → builtin fallback\n19: - Multiple files via CLI args open as tabs\n20: \n21: **Not Implemented:**\n22: - File dialogs (Save As, Open File, Open Folder)\n23: - Visual feedback during file drag-hover\n24: - text_x) / char_width).floor() as usize;\n73:         let status_bar_height = line_height;\n74:         let visible_lines = (window_height as usize)\n75:             .saturating_sub(status_bar_height) / line_height;\n76: \n77:         Self { window_width, window_height, line_height, char_width, visible_lines, visible_columns }\n78:     }\n79: }\n80: ```\n[... omitted lines 81 to 806 ...]",
